# Seed 71 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the first run with the applications in the selection: a loophole found (Iteration 516)

`seed 71 10h widths noevolved then experiment footprint.py ... then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment
appbench.py --zip ~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16` at b5e9cbd (Iteration 498). 32,733 designs, generations 0-130
(24,049 alive, 8,678 screened); islands off. The front 210 designs (154 by mutation, 30 borrowed, 25 by crossover).
**A speed break**: the speed is over seven workloads from this run (apps the seventh) - it compares with no run before
it; sizes, memory and everything within the run do.

**The applications in the selection.** Fewer designs an hour: 3,273 against seed 70's 3,616 (-9.5%), the seventh
workload's cost. The fastest, f83a64a706, at 0.1426 over seven - its six old workloads 0.1193, seed 70's fastest 0.1204:
the old speed kept. appbench.py on 16 of the front: in-run speed against the applications' geometric mean, Spearman
**0.861** (seed 70: 0.553) - the selection now sees what the applications do; the fastest's applications 0.491 of s6
(seed 70's: 0.756). The JIT genes: jitmul on 72 of the front's 130 JIT designs, jitcfa 9, jitexec 2, jitdoes none, jitrp
3 - jitmul taken up, the others not.

**The loophole.** measure() timed the workloads and never compared what they printed. 791 cpt16 designs stopped early in
the applications on undefined words - their apps time 0.13 of s6 against ~1.07 on the other six - and one of them,
908baebc14, reached the front (appbench: wrong on gc16, fcp, brainless and cd16sim). Fixed for seed 72 (the
after-seed-71 batch's sixth patch): every workload's output compared with s6's from the same rounds.
And jitrp with lto did not compile - six died, "global register variable follows a function definition": kept apart.

Facts: held out, sieve's Spearman 0.956 (fastest third 0.557, tenth 0.170), regret +6.1%; the fast end's optimism
1.103 (the tenth); **32-bit failures none** (seed 70: 906 - the CELL+/CELLS fix); the smallest 14,210 bytes; remote
checks dream 0.804 (default, 24 designs), 0.969 (32-bit), 0.966 (64-bit), the Tegra 0.607 (7 designs).

---

# Evolved VM designs

24055 designs evaluated, 24049 alive, 8678 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 0fffbf7a52, speed 0.786, binary + image 25,240, memory 140 KB - Q 0.256 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
